-\int_{\Omega}v\cdot(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)=\int_{\Omega}(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v)h+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{l}\tilde{a}^{li}v_{i}h\,dy\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)|h|^{2}\,dy+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h|h|^{2}+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{l}\tilde{a}^{li}v_{i}h\,dy,

and

\int_{\Omega}\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\eta\cdot v\,dy=\sum_{j=1}^{3}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta|^{2}\,dy+\int_{\Omega}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi)\,dy-\int_{\Omega}(\text{div }\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j})(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\cdot v\,dy.

Therefore

\frac{d}{dt}\left(\|v\|_{0}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}h\|_{0}^{2}\right)\lesssim P(E_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.24)

As for
h
, one has
\|h(T)\|_{0}\lesssim\|\mathfrak{h}_{0}\|_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}h(t)\|_{0}\,dt
to control its
L^{2}
norm.

Boundary control: Reduce to divergence and interior tangential estimates

The boundary part can be reduced to interior tangential estimates and divergence by Lemma
2.3
.

\left|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}v\cdot N\right|_{-1/2}\lesssim\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}v\right\|_{0}+\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{3}\text{div }v\right\|_{0}.

(3.25)

Similarly,

\left|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot N\right|_{-1/2}\lesssim\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}+\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{3}\text{div }\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}.

(3.26)

Curl control: Direct computation

First, it suffices to control the Eulerian curl due to the a priori assumption (
3.7
)

\|\text{curl }X\|_{3}\leq\|\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}X\|_{3}+\|\text{Id}-\tilde{a}\|_{2}\|X\|_{4}\lesssim\|\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}X\|_{3}+\delta\|X\|_{4},

where the
\delta
-term can be absorbed by
\|X\|_{4}
in the energy functional.

Recall in (
3.2
) that we have
\partial_{t}\eta=v+\psi
and
\partial_{t}v-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\eta=-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h
. Taking (Eulerian curl)
\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}
, we get the evolution equation of
\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v
.

\partial_{t}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)=\underbrace{\text{curl }_{\tilde{a}_{t}}v+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\left[\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta}_{J_{0}}.

(3.27)

Then we take 3 spatial derivatives to get

\partial_{t}\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial^{3}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)=\partial^{3}J_{0}+\underbrace{\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\left[\partial^{3},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)}_{J_{1}}.

(3.28)

Now we compute the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
3.28
) with
\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v
to get

\int_{\Omega}\left(\partial_{t}\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)\,dy=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right|^{2}\,dy.

(3.29)

Then we integrate
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
by parts in the second term of LHS in (
3.28
). Note that the boundary term vanishes because
\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot N=0
for
j=1,2,3
.

\displaystyle-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial^{3}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)\cdot\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)\,dy

(3.30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)\cdot\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)v\right)\,dy

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(\text{div }\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j})\partial^{3}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)\cdot\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)\,dy}_{J_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\left(\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)\cdot\left[(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial),\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}\right]v\,dy}_{J_{3}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\,dy-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\,dy

\displaystyle+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\partial^{3}\left(\left[\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\,dy+J_{2}+J_{3}

It remains to control the terms
J_{0}\sim J_{5}
where

\displaystyle J_{4}:=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\,dy,

(3.31)

\displaystyle J_{5}:=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\partial^{3}\left(\left[\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\,dy

(3.32)

These commutators can be controlled either by direct computation or using Lemma
3.2
. We have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(\partial^{3}J_{0}+J_{1})\cdot\left(\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right)\,dy\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}P(\|\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{4},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}.\|\partial v\|_{4},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3})\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.33)

For
J_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}\|v\|_{4}.

(3.34)

For
J_{3}
, direct computation shows that

\displaystyle J_{3}\lesssim P\left(\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3}\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.35)

By using Lemma
3.2
, we have that

\displaystyle J_{4}+J_{5}\lesssim P\left(\|\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{4},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\partial_{t}\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\overline{\partial}{}\psi\|_{4}\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.36)

Summing up (
3.29
), (
3.30
), (
3.33
)-(
3.36
), we get the Eulerian curl estimates by

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left(\int_{\Omega}\left|\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}v\right|^{2}\,dy+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left|\partial^{3}\text{curl}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\,dy\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)),

(3.37)

and thus for sufficiently small
\delta>0
we have

\left\|\text{curl }v(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}+\left\|\text{curl }(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2}\left(\|v\|_{4}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}^{2}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.38)

Divergence estimates: Reduce to the elliptic estimates of
h

By the third equation of (
3.2
) and the a priori assumption (
3.7
), we know

\left\|\text{div }v\right\|_{3}^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2}\|v\|_{4}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\right\|_{3}^{2}.

(3.39)

As for the divergence of the deformation tensor
\mathbf{F}
, we recall that the original system (
1.11
) reads
\text{div }_{a}\mathbf{F}=-e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h
. Now we should re-produce a silimar formula of
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
for the smoothed-out problem (
3.2
) up to some error terms. Direct computation together with
\partial_{t}\eta=v+\psi
yields that

\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)-\text{div }\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}=\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)v\,dt+\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi+\text{div }_{\tilde{a}_{t}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\,dt

(3.40)

Recall that
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v=-\partial_{t}e(h)
, so we commute
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}
with
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
to get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)v\,dt=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{t}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)e(h)\right)\,dt+\int_{0}^{T}\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]v\,dt

(3.41)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\bigg|_{0}^{T}+\int_{0}^{T}\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]v\,dt.

Summing up these two equalities, we get

\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)=-e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi+\text{div }_{\tilde{a}_{t}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta+\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]v\,dt.

(3.42)

Direct computation shows that the commutator is only one term with first order derivative. One can also equivalently compute in Eulerian coordinate.

\displaystyle\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]f=

\displaystyle\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\left(\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}f_{i}\right)-\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}\left(\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}f_{i}\right)

(3.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}f_{i}-\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}f_{i}=\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}f_{i}+\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\tilde{a}^{lr}\partial_{k}\partial_{m}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{mi}\partial_{l}f_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{a}^{mi}\partial_{m}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\tilde{a}^{lr}\partial_{l}f_{i}-\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}f_{i}+\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}_{0}^{kj}\partial_{k}f_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}f_{i}.

On the other hand, we compute that

\displaystyle\text{div }_{\tilde{a}_{t}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta=-\tilde{a}^{lr}\partial_{m}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{mi}\partial_{l}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i})=-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}.

Therefore we get

\displaystyle\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)=

\displaystyle-e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}\,dt

(3.44)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)+\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\psi_{r})\,dt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left(\partial_{t}\eta_{i}-\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{i}\right)+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}-(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}\,dt

Remark
.

We find that, if
\kappa=0
(and thus
\psi=0,~\tilde{\eta}=\eta
), then the formula (
3.44
) for the smoothed-Eulerian divergence of
\mathbf{F}
exactly reproduces the divergence constraint for the original equation.

By Jensen’s inequality, we know

\displaystyle\left\|\text{div }(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}\lesssim\delta^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{4}^{2}+\left\|\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}

(3.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{4}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\right\|_{3}^{2}+\left\|\text{div }_{\tilde{a}_{t}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{3}^{2}+\left\|\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]v\right\|_{3}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{4}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\|_{4},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\partial_{t}\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\partial v\|_{3}\right)\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{4}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h(T)\right\|_{3}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

Therefore, the divergence control of
v
and
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
are reduced to weight norm

\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\|_{3}\text{ and }\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{3}

which will be further reduced to tangentially-differentiated wave equation of
h
by using Christodoulou-Lindblad elliptic estimates Lemma
2.6
. We postpone the proof to Section
3.3
and
3.4
.

3.2
Tangential estimates of full spatial derivatives: Alinhac good unknown

The boundary part in the Hodge-type decomposition is reduced to the interior tangential estimates and divergence control. We already reduce divergence control to the elliptic estimates of
h
. This part we deal with the interior tangential derivatives
\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}v\|_{0}
and
\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{0}
. Due to the special structure of the correction term
\psi
, we replace
\overline{\partial}{}^{4}
by
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}
. Note that we cannot directly commute
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}
in the equation
\partial_{t}v+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\eta=-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h
because one of the leading order terms in the commutator
[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},\nabla_{\tilde{a}}]h
is
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{a}\cdot\partial h\approx\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial\tilde{\eta}\times\partial\tilde{\eta}\cdot\partial h
which cannot be controlled in
L^{2}
. The reason is that the essential highest order term in the standard derivatives of a covariant derivative is actually the covariant derivative of Alinhac good unknown, instead of simply commuting
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}
with
\nabla_{\tilde{a}}
.

For a function
f
, define its Alinhac good unknown to be
\mathbf{f}:=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}f-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}f
. Then the following property holds

\displaystyle\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}f)

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{\alpha}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}f)+(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{a}^{li})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},\tilde{a}^{li},\partial_{l}f]

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}f)-\overline{\partial}{}\overline{\Delta}{}(\tilde{a}^{lr}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{mi})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},\tilde{a}^{li},\partial_{l}f]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}f-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\eta_{r}\tilde{a}^{lr}\partial_{l}f)}_{=\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\mathbf{f}}+\underbrace{\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\eta_{r}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}f)-([\overline{\partial}{}\overline{\Delta}{},\tilde{a}^{lr}\tilde{a}^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\tilde{\eta}_{r})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},\tilde{a}^{li},\partial_{l}f]}_{=:C^{i}(f)},

where
[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},g,h]:=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(gh)-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(g)h-g\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(h)
.
Direct computation yields that

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\eta_{r}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}f)\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{4}\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}f)\|_{L^{\infty}}\;

\displaystyle\|([\overline{\partial}{}\overline{\Delta}{},\tilde{a}^{lr}\tilde{a}^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\tilde{\eta}_{r})\partial_{l}f\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|[\overline{\partial}{}\overline{\Delta}{},\tilde{a}^{lr}\tilde{a}^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\|_{0}\|f\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\lesssim P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{4})\|f\|_{3}

\displaystyle\|[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},\tilde{a}^{li},\partial_{l}f]\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{4})\|f\|_{4}.

Therefore, Alinhac good unknown enjoys the following important properties:

\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}f)=\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\mathbf{f}+C^{\alpha}(f)

(3.46)

with

\|C^{i}(f)\|\lesssim P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{4})\|f\|_{4}.

(3.47)

We introduce the Alinhac good unknowns of
v
and
h
with respect to
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}
by

\mathbf{V}:=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}v-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v,~~\mathbf{H}:=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h.

Taking
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}
in the second equation of (
3.2
), we get the evolution of Alinhac good unknowns

\partial_{t}\mathbf{V}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\mathbf{H}-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)=\underbrace{\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v)-C(h)+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\left[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)}_{K_{0}},

(3.48)

subjected to

\displaystyle\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\cdot\mathbf{V}=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v)-C^{i}(v_{i})~~~

\displaystyle\text{in }\Omega

(3.49)

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}=(-\partial_{3}h)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}~~~

\displaystyle\text{on }\Gamma.

(3.50)

Now we take
L^{2}
-inner product of (
3.48
) and
\mathbf{V}
to get

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|\mathbf{V}\right|^{2}\,dy=-\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\mathbf{H}\cdot\mathbf{V}\,dy+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right)\mathbf{V}\,dy+\int_{\Omega}K_{0}\cdot\mathbf{V}\,dy

(3.51)

For the first term on the RHS of (
3.51
), we integrate by parts and invoke (
3.49
)-(
3.50
) to get

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\mathbf{H}\cdot\mathbf{V}=-\int_{\Omega}\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}\mathbf{H}\cdot\mathbf{V}_{i}\,dy

(3.52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\mathbf{H}(\tilde{a}^{li}N_{l}\mathbf{V}_{i})dS+\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\cdot\mathbf{V})\,dy+\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{\mu}\tilde{a}^{li})\mathbf{H}\mathbf{V}_{i}\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\partial_{3}h\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{li}N_{l}\mathbf{V}_{i}dS+\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v)\,dy-\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}C^{i}(v_{i})+\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{l}\tilde{a}^{li})\mathbf{H}\mathbf{V}_{\alpha}\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(-\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\mathbf{V}_{i}dS+\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v)\,dy-\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}C^{i}(v_{i})+\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{l}\tilde{a}^{li})\mathbf{H}\mathbf{V}_{i}\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:B+K_{1}+K_{2}+K_{3}.

We postpone the estimates of
B
to the end of this section because it is the most difficult part and contributes to the boundary energy in
\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)
. The term
K_{2},K_{3}
can be controlled directly

\displaystyle K_{2}=-\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}C^{i}(v_{i})\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{H}\|_{0}\|C(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P\left(\|h\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3}\right).

(3.53)

\displaystyle K_{3}=\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{l}\tilde{a}^{li})\mathbf{H}\mathbf{V}_{i}\,dy\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial\tilde{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathbf{H}\|_{0}\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}\lesssim P\left(\|h\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3}\right).

(3.54)

In
K_{1}
, we invoke
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v=-e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h
to get

\displaystyle K_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}v)\,dy=\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h)\,dy

(3.55)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\left|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\right|^{2}\,dy+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\right|^{2}\,dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}\mathbf{H}\left(\left[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},e^{\prime}(h)\right]\partial_{t}h\right)\,dy\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}h\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\,dy}_{K_{11}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\left|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\right|^{2}\,dy+K_{11}+P\left(\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|h\|_{4},\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\|_{3}\right)

The term
K_{11}
should be controlled by integrating
\partial_{t}
by parts under the time integral.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}K_{11}(t)\,dt=-

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}h(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)\,dy\,dt

(3.56)

\displaystyle\overset{\partial_{t}}{=}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)\,dy\bigg|^{t=T}_{t=0}+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\partial_{t}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\right\|_{0}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\eta\|_{0}\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg|_{t=T}+P\left(\|\mathfrak{h}_{0}\|_{4},\|v_{0}\|_{4}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|h\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4}\right)\,dt.

Invoking that
\partial^{2}\eta(0)=0
(recall
\eta(0,y)=y
is identity map initially), we have

\displaystyle\|e^{\prime}(h)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\|_{0}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\eta\|_{0}\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T))\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\partial_{t}\eta(t)\right\|_{0}^{2}\,dt

Remark
.

One can also use Young’s inequality to control
\int_{0}^{T}K_{11}
by

\delta\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h(T)\|_{0}^{2}+\left(\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\|v\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|h\|_{3},\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}h\|_{3})\,dt\right).

Therefore we get the estimate of
K_{1}
:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}K_{1}(t)\,dt\lesssim-\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h(T)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.57)

Next we estimate the tangential derivative of
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
produced in the second term on RHS of (
3.51
). Integrating
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
by parts yields that

\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right)\mathbf{V}\,dy=-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\mathbf{V}\,dy\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}(\text{div }\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j})\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\mathbf{V}}_{K_{4}}

K_{4}
has direct control

K_{4}\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\partial\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{0}\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{0}\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}P\left(\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\tilde{a}\|_{3},\|\partial\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right).

(3.58)

In the remaining term, we invoke
\mathbf{V}=\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}v-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v
and
v=\partial_{t}\eta-\psi
to get

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\mathbf{V}\,dy

(3.59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta^{i}\right)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}v_{i}-\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v_{i}\right)\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta^{i}\right)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\right)\,dy+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\right)\,dy

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta^{i}\right)\left(\left[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right]v_{i}+(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v_{i}\right)\right)\,dy

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\,dy+K_{5},

where the commutators
K_{5}
can be directly controlled with the help of Lemma
3.2

K_{5}\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{4}P\left(\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\|_{4},\|\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{4},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}\|_{4}\right)\lesssim P\left(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)\right).

(3.60)

Summing up (
3.58
)-(
3.60
), we get

\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right)\mathbf{V}\,dy\lesssim-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\,dy+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.61)

Also, the last term in (
3.51
) can also be directly controlled

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}K_{0}\cdot\mathbf{V}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v)\|_{0}+\|C(h)\|_{0}+\|[\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{0}\right)\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{0}

(3.62)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P\left(\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\partial_{t}v\|_{3},\|h\|_{4},\|\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{4},\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

It remains to deal with the boundary term
B
produced in (
3.52
). In the proof we will see that the boundary integral together with the Raylrigh-Taylor sign condition contributes to the boundary energy, which enters to the highest order and exactly gives the control of the second fundamental form of the free surface. The correction term, first introduced by Gu-Wang
[
31
]
, plays the key role in eliminating the higher order terms brought by the tangential smoothing. First, we have

\displaystyle B=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(-\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\mathbf{V}_{i}~dS

(3.63)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}~dS

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\psi_{i}\,dS-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v_{i}\,dS

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3}.

The term
B_{1}
is expected to give the boundary part of the energy functional
\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)
after moving one mollifier from
\tilde{\eta}_{k}
to
\eta_{i}
.

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}~dS

(3.64)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}~dS\underbrace{+\int_{\Gamma}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\left(\left[\Lambda_{\kappa},\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\right]\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\right)\,dS}_{B_{11}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Gamma}\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,dS+B_{11}

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,dS}_{B_{12}}\underbrace{-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\partial_{t}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS}_{B_{13}}.

The time integral of the main term gives the boundary part of
\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)
with the help of Taylor sign condition (
1.8
), (
3.6
).

\int_{0}^{T}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Gamma}\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,dS\right)\,dt\leq-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\Lambda_{\kappa}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|^{T}_{0}.

(3.65)

B_{11}
and
B_{12}
can be controlled with the help of mollifier property (Lemma
2.4
).

\displaystyle B_{11}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\overline{\partial}{}^{1.5}\overline{\Delta}{}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\overline{\partial}{}^{0.5}\left(\left[\Lambda_{\kappa},\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\right]\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\right)\,dS

(3.66)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\eta_{k}\|_{4}\left|\partial_{3}h\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\right|_{W^{1,\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\|_{4}\lesssim P\left(\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{3},\|h\|_{4}\right),

B_{12}\lesssim\left|\partial_{t}\partial_{3}h\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}h\|_{3}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}.

(3.67)

For
B_{13}
, we invoke
\partial_{t}\tilde{a}^{3i}=-\tilde{a}^{3r}\partial_{m}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{mi}
to get

\displaystyle B_{13}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3r}\partial_{m}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{mi}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS

(3.68)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3r}\partial_{3}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3r}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{r}\tilde{a}^{Li}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3r}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}\psi_{r}\tilde{a}^{Li}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle B_{131}+B_{132}+B_{133}.

Among these three terms,
B_{132}
cannot be directly controlled. Instead, it will cancel with the main term in
B_{3}
and the correction term
B_{2}
. First we give the control of
B_{131}

B_{131}\lesssim\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\left|\partial_{3}h\tilde{a}^{3r}\partial_{3}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.69)

In
B_{133}
, we need to use the mollifier property Lemma
2.4
to control
\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta
by sacrificing a
1/\sqrt{\kappa}
. Luckily, the
\psi
term compensates this factor such that the energy bound is still uniform in
\kappa
.

B_{133}\lesssim\sum_{L=1}^{2}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\kappa}}\left|\partial_{3}h\tilde{a}^{3r}\tilde{a}^{Li}\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}\left|\overline{\partial}{}\Lambda_{\kappa}\psi\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\eta\right|_{7/2}.

(3.70)

Then we use Sobolev embedding
W^{1,4}(\mathbb{T}^{2})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})
to get

\left|\overline{\partial}{}\psi\right|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\left|\overline{\Delta}{}\psi\right|_{L^{4}}=\sum_{L=1}^{2}\left|\overline{\Delta}{}\left(\eta_{k}-\tilde{\eta}_{k}\right)\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}v-\overline{\Delta}{}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\left(v-\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v\right)\right|_{L^{4}}.

By the mollifier property (
2.8
), we know

\left|\overline{\partial}{}\psi\right|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa}P\left(\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{3}\right),

and thus

B_{133}\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.71)

Now we start to analyze
B_{3}:=-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k})\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}(\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}\cdot\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v_{i})\,dS
. Again we separate normal derivative from tangential derivative.

\displaystyle B_{3}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{3r}\partial_{3}v_{i}\,dS

(3.72)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{Lr}\partial_{L}v_{i}\,dS}_{B_{31}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}P\left(\|v\|_{3},\|h\|_{3},\|\eta\|_{4}\right)+B_{31}.

Here we note that
\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{i}
can be controlled by commuting one mollifier with the help of Lemma
2.4

\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{i}\right|_{0}\leq\left|\Lambda_{\kappa}\left(\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right)\right|_{0}+\left|\left[\Lambda_{\kappa},\tilde{a}^{3k}\right]\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\right|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{4}).

There are still 3 terms remaining to be controlled

\displaystyle B_{2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\psi_{i}\,dS

(3.73)

\displaystyle B_{132}=

\displaystyle\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{3r}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{r}\tilde{a}^{Li}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\,dS

(3.74)

\displaystyle B_{31}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i}\,dS

(3.75)

We plug the expression of
\overline{\Delta}{}\psi
in
B_{2}
to get

\displaystyle B_{2}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\overline{\partial}{}^{2}(\overline{\Delta}{}\eta_{r}\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{i})\,dS

(3.76)

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\overline{\partial}{}^{2}\tilde{\eta}_{r}\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i}\,dS

(3.77)

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}([\overline{\partial}{}^{2},\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i}]\overline{\Delta}{}\tilde{\eta}_{r})\,dS

(3.78)

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\overline{\partial}{}^{2}\mathbb{P}_{=0}\left(\overline{\Delta}{}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v-\overline{\Delta}{}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v\right).

(3.79)

First we notice that
\eqref{B202}+B_{31}=0
, and (
3.78
)-(
3.79
) can be controlled by direct computation. Notice that the zero frequency part is always of lower order by Bernstein’s inequality.

\displaystyle\eqref{B203}\lesssim

\displaystyle P\left(\|h\|_{3},\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)),

(3.80)

\displaystyle\eqref{B204}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{L=1}^{2}\left|\partial_{3}h\tilde{a}^{3i}\right|_{L^{\infty}}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}\left|\overline{\Delta}{}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v-\overline{\Delta}{}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}\eta_{k}\tilde{a}^{Lk}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v\right|_{0}\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)).

(3.81)

In order for cancelling
B_{132}
, we just need to commute one mollifier in
B_{201}
from
\eta_{k}
to
\eta_{i}
. We find that (
3.82
) exactly canceles with
B_{132}
. All the commutators (
3.83
)-(
3.84
) can be controlled directly so we omit the details.

(
3.76
)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\left(\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{i}\right)\left(\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{r}\right)\,dS

(3.82)

\displaystyle~~~~-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{k}\left(\left[\Lambda_{\kappa},\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{i}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{i}\right]\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\eta_{r}\right)\,dS

(3.83)

\displaystyle~~~~-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial h}{\partial N}\right)\tilde{a}^{3i}\tilde{a}^{3k}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\tilde{\eta}_{k}\left(\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{2},\tilde{a}^{Lr}\overline{\partial}{}_{i}\Lambda_{\kappa}^{2}v_{i}\right]\overline{\Delta}{}\eta_{r}\right)\,dS.

(3.84)

Summarising (
3.63
)-(
3.84
), we get the control of the boundary integral
B
by

\int_{0}^{T}B(t)\,dt\lesssim-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.85)

Now, summing up (
3.53
), (
3.54
), (
3.57
), (
3.61
), (
3.62
), (
3.63
) and (
3.85
), we get the estimates of Alinhac good unknowns

\left\|\mathbf{V}\right\|_{0}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}h\right\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.86)

Finally by the definition of Alinhac good unknown, we know

\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}v\|_{0}\lesssim\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}+\|\overline{\partial}^{2}\overline{\Delta}\eta\|_{0}\|\tilde{a}\partial v\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}+P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt,

and thus we finalize the tangential estimates

\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}v\right\|_{0}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{4}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\overline{\partial}{}^{4}h\right\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|\tilde{a}^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{4}\Lambda_{\kappa}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.87)

3.3
Elliptic estimates and reduction to tangentially-differentiated wave equations

The divergence of
v
and
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
are reduced to
\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\|_{3}
and
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{3}
in div-curl estimates. In this section we invoke Christodoulou-Lindblad type elliptic estimates (Lemma
2.6
) to further reduce the estimates of
h
to tangential derivatives.

Let us derive the wave equation of
h
first. Taking smoothed-divergence
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}
in the second equation of (
3.2
), we get

e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h-\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h=-\partial_{t}\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}v_{i}-e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2}-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)-\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right).

(3.88)

Invoking the formula (
3.44
) of
\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
, we know the wave equation of
h
becomes

\displaystyle e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h-\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h=

\displaystyle-\partial_{t}\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}v_{i}-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{l}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{l}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{i}\right)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h

(3.89)

\displaystyle-e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}\,dt.

Remark
.

Among all terms in the source part of (
3.89
), the first two lines show the terms that also appears in the original system (
1.14
). In Eulerian coordinates, they can be written as

(\nabla v)\cdot(\nabla v)-(\nabla\mathbf{F})\cdot(\nabla\mathbf{F})+\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime}(\mathfrak{h})(\mathbf{F}^{j}\cdot\nabla)^{2}\mathfrak{h}-e^{\prime\prime}(\mathfrak{h})(D_{t}\mathfrak{h})^{2}+e^{\prime\prime}(\mathfrak{h})\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{j}\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{h}\right)^{2}.

The last two terms are of higher order weight function and thus much are smaller. Therefore the main terms are the first three terms.

Now we start to control
h
. From the a priori assumption (
3.7
) and Poincaré inequality, we have

\|h\|_{4}\lesssim\|\partial^{4}h\|_{0}\leq\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{3}+\|a-I_{3}\|_{3}\|h\|_{4}\lesssim\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{3}+\delta\|h\|_{4},

and the
\delta
-term can be absorbed by LHS if we choose
\delta>0
to be sufficiently small. From now on, we will use the notation
\|f\|_{r}\approx\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}f\|_{r-1}
to record similar inequality for
1\leq r\leq 4
.

Invoking Lemma
2.6
, we have

\|h\|_{4}\approx\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{3}\lesssim P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{3})\left(\|\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{2}+\|\overline{\partial}{}\tilde{\eta}\|_{3}\|h\|_{3}\right).

(3.90)

Then inserting the wave equation, we know

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{2}

(3.91)

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial_{t}\tilde{a}\cdot\partial v\right\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2}\right\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)^{2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{T}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}\right)\right\|_{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial v\|_{2}^{2}\|\partial\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\tilde{a}\|_{2}^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\right\|_{3}^{2}+\left\|\left(e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\left(e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|\tilde{a}\|_{3},\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\|_{4}+\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}\|_{4}+\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{4}\right)\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

Therefore, we reduce the estimates of
\|h\|_{4}
to
\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\|_{2}
and
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{2}
.

Remark
.

1.

In (
3.90
), the essential terms are
\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\|_{2}
and
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{2}
obtained from
\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h
. The term
P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{3})\|\overline{\partial}{}\tilde{\eta}\|_{3}\|h\|_{3}
is actually a lower order term. One can repeatedly apply Lemma
2.6
to
h
in order to reduce to the estimates of
\|h\|_{1}
which can be directly controlled by the
L^{2}
-estimate of the wave equation (
3.89
). Due to this reason, we will omit the processes of controlling these lower order terms appearing in the elliptic estimates throughout this manuscript.

2.

Note that
h|_{\Gamma}=0
and
\partial_{t},(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
are tangential derivatives on the boundary, so
\partial_{t}^{2}h
and
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h
are still vanishing on the boundary and thus we can use Lemma
2.6
to further reduce them to full tangentially-differentiated quantites and use the tangentially-differentiated wave equation to finalize the control. We postpone the proof after the elliptic estimates of
\partial_{t}h
.

Next we control
\|\partial_{t}h\|_{3}
in the same way by inserting the
\partial_{t}
-differentiated wave equation (
3.89
)

\|\partial_{t}h\|_{3}\approx\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h\|_{2}\lesssim P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{2})\left(\|\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h\|_{1}+\|\overline{\partial}{}\tilde{\eta}\|_{2}\|\partial_{t}h\|_{2}\right),

(3.92)

and then

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{1}+\|[\Delta_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}]h\|_{1}

(3.93)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h\right\|_{1}+\left\|e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\tilde{a}\partial v)\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\partial_{t}\left(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)\right\|_{1}^{2}+\|[\Delta_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}]h\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}(e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2})\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|\partial_{t}\left(e^{\prime\prime}(h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)^{2}\right)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\left(\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}\right)\right\|_{1}.

Notice that
|e^{(k)}(h)|\lesssim|e^{\prime}(h)|^{k}
, so the power of weight function
e^{\prime}(h)
is always enough. For example,

\left|\partial_{t}(e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2})\right|\leq\left|2(e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h)\partial_{t}h\right|+\left|e^{\prime\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{3}\right|\lesssim\left|((e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}h)\partial_{t}h\right|+\left|(e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h)^{3}\right|.

Therefore, we know

\|\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h\|_{1}\lesssim\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h\right\|_{1}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

(3.94)

As one can see, the remaining terms in (
3.94
) are exactly from the energy functionalsof
\partial_{t}^{3}
-differentiated and
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}
-differentiated wave equation (
3.89
).

Similarly,
\|\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{3}\approx\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{3}
can be controlled by Lemma
2.6
and inserting
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated wave equation. We omit the details and get the following conclusion

\|\text{div }(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{3}\lesssim\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right\|_{1}+\sum_{k=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right\|_{1}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt,

(3.95)

where the remaining terms will be controlled by analyzing
\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated and
\sum\limits_{j,k=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated wave equation (
3.89
).

Now we come to further reduce the remaining terms in (
3.90
) to full tangential derivatives.

We have

\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{2}\approx\left\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\left(\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right)\right\|_{1}\leq\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{1}+\left\|(\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)})^{-1/2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{1},

(3.96)

and then

\displaystyle\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{2})\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{0}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt

(3.97)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{2})\left(\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{0}\right)+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

Similarly we have

\displaystyle\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{2}\approx

\displaystyle\|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{1}+\|e^{\prime\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{1}

(3.98)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{2})\|e^{\prime}(h)\Delta_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{0}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{2})\left(\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{0}+\sum_{k=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{0}\right)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt.

Summarizing (
3.94
), (
3.95
), (
3.96
) and (
3.98
), we still need to control the following quantities.

\displaystyle\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{1}+\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\|_{0}

(3.99)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\|_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right\|_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j,k=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right\|_{0}+\sum_{k=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right\|_{1}.

Remark
.

The quantites in (
3.99
) exactly correspond to the weighted energy functionals of
\partial_{t}^{3}
-differentiated,
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated,
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{t}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}
-differentiated and
\sum\limits_{j,k=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated wave equation of
h
(
3.89
). Keep in mind that these derivatives are all tangential, and thus there is no boundary integral appearing in the energy estimates.

Reduction of the remaining terms in the energy functional
\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)

Comparing with the energy functional
\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(T)
, we still need to control

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}v\|_{3},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\eta\|_{3},\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{2},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{2},

(3.100)

\displaystyle\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1},\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\|_{1},\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{0},\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{4}\eta\|_{0}.

Let us start with full time derivatives. Recall
\partial_{t}v=-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\eta
gives

\partial_{t}^{4}v=-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h+[\nabla_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}^{3}]h+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}\eta,

and thus

\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{4}v\|_{0}\lesssim\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\right\|_{1}+\text{lower order terms}.

(3.101)

Then

\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{4}\eta\|_{0}\lesssim\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}+\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\psi\|_{0}.

(3.102)

Recall that in (
3.12
) we only need at most 3 time derivatives of
v
to control
e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\psi
. Therefore, the control of full time derivatives of
v
and
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta
has been reduced to less time derivatives. Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h\|_{1}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{2}\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{2}+\text{lower order terms}.

(3.103)

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\|_{1}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{2}\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{2}+\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}\psi\|_{1}.

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{2}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\eta\|_{3}+\text{lower order terms}.

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{2}\|\partial_{t}v\|_{3}+\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\psi\|_{2}.

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}v\|_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h\|_{3}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{3}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}.

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\eta\|_{3}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\|_{3}\|v\|_{4}+\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\|_{4}.

Invoking Lemma
3.2
, all these time derivatives are reduced to full spatial derivatives and the estimates of
h
. Since the estimates of
\|v\|_{4}
and
\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}
are reduced to the elliptic estimates of
h
, we know it remains to control the quantities in (
3.99
), which will be controlled via tangentially-differentiated wave equation of
h
, and thus can be controlled by
\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t))\,dt
.

3.4
Special Structure of the wave equation

This part is the key to the whole proof to establish the energy estimates. Let us start with the wave equation of
h
(
3.89
).

\displaystyle e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h-\Delta_{\tilde{a}}h=

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\partial_{t}\tilde{a}^{li}\partial_{l}v_{i}-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{l}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{l}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{i}\right)}_{L_{1}}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h

(3.104)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)^{2}}_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}\underbrace{\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi-\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta_{i}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}_{r}+\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{i}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}_{r}\right)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}^{r}v_{i}}_{L_{3}^{j}}\,dt.

We will show the full details in the case of
\partial_{t}^{3}
-differentiated wave equation and
\sum\limits_{j,k=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated wave equation, which are the most complicated cases. The other mixed cases follow in the same way so we will omit those details.

3.4.1
Fully time-differentiated wave equation

First, we differentiate
\partial_{t}^{3}
in the wave equation (
3.89
) to get

\displaystyle e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{5}h-\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}h

(3.105)

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left[e^{\prime}(h),\partial_{t}^{3}\right]\partial_{t}^{2}h+\left[\partial_{t}^{3},\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\right]h+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left[\partial_{t}^{3},e^{\prime}(h)\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h}_{L_{41}}

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}^{3}(L_{1}+L_{2})-\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{t}^{3}L_{3}^{j}\,dt.

Then we take
L^{2}
-inner product of (
3.105
) with
(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{4}h
to get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}\partial_{t}^{5}h\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy-\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

(3.106)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}h\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\left(L_{41}+\partial_{t}^{3}(L_{1}+L_{2})\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{4}h\cdot\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{t}^{3}L_{3}^{j}\,dt\right)\,dy.

The LHS of (
3.106
) gives the weighted energy of
\partial_{t}^{4}h
and
\partial_{t}^{3}h
. We integrate by parts in the second term to get

\displaystyle\text{LHS of }\eqref{ttt1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right|^{2}\,dt+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy

(3.107)

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\partial_{t}^{4}h\right|^{2}\,dy-\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy

\displaystyle+2\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)\cdot(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy+\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\cdot\left([\nabla_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}]\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\,dy

The first term on RHS of (
3.106
) gives the weighted energy of
\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h
after integrating
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
by parts

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}h\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

(3.108)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy-3\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}^{0}_{lj}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

The remaining terms can all be directly controlled by invoking the physical conditions
|e^{(k)}(h)|\lesssim|e^{\prime}(h)|^{k}
. We list the details as follow:

In (
3.107
):

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\partial_{t}^{4}h\right|^{2}\,dy-\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy

(3.109)

\displaystyle+2\int_{\Omega}e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)\cdot(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy+\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\cdot\left([\nabla_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}]\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{0}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\tilde{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}}\|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\|_{0}\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\|_{0}+\|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{0}^{2}.

In (
3.108
):

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right|^{2}\,dy-3\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\right)\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

(3.110)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{l}\mathbf{F}^{0}_{lj}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\|\partial\mathbf{F}^{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{0}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}

In (
3.106
),

\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\left(L_{41}+\partial_{t}^{3}(L_{1}+L_{2})\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy\lesssim\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\left(L_{41}+\partial_{t}^{3}(L_{1}+L_{2})\right)\right\|_{0}.

We compute that

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{3}L_{1}\|_{0}\lesssim\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{3}v\|_{0}\|\partial v\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{0}\|\partial_{t}v\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\|\tilde{a}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\left(\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}\tilde{\eta}\|_{0}\|\partial(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}\|_{0}\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{3}L_{2}=

\displaystyle e^{(5)}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{5}+8e^{(4)}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{3}\partial_{t}^{2}h+12e^{(3)}(h)\partial_{t}h(\partial_{t}^{2}h)^{2}+4e^{(3)}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}h

\displaystyle+6e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}h\partial_{t}^{3}h+2e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\partial_{t}^{4}h

\displaystyle+e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left(2((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h)+6((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h)\right)

\displaystyle+e^{(3)}(h)\big(\partial_{t}^{3}h((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)^{2}+\partial_{t}^{2}h((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h)

\displaystyle~~~~+\partial_{t}h(2((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h)^{2}+2((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}h))\big)

\displaystyle+e^{(4)}(h)\left(2(\partial_{t}h)^{2}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h)+2(\partial_{t}h)(\partial_{t}^{2}h)((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+e^{(5)}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{3}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h)^{2}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{3}L_{2}\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle P\bigg(\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\partial_{t}h\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}^{2}h\|_{2},\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\|_{1},\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\|_{0}

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{L^{\infty}},\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\partial_{t}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{2},\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{1},\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)h\|_{0}\bigg),

and
L_{41}
consists of quadratic and cubic terms and each term containing 5 derivatives in total with highest order equal to 4. Here we list the precise form, but analogous computations will be omitted in the remaining of the paper.

\displaystyle L_{41}=

\displaystyle\left[e^{\prime}(h),\partial_{t}^{3}\right]\partial_{t}^{2}h+\left[\partial_{t}^{3},\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\right]h+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left[\partial_{t}^{3},e^{\prime}(h)\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\prime}(h)(3\partial_{t}h\partial_{t}^{4}h+4\partial_{t}^{2}h\partial_{t}^{3}h)+3e^{\prime\prime}(h)((\partial_{t}h)^{2}\partial_{t}^{3}h+\partial_{t}h(\partial_{t}^{2}h)^{2})+e^{\prime\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{3}

\displaystyle+(\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)+(\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h)+(\Delta_{\tilde{a}}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h)

\displaystyle+(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}h)+(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}h)+(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{2}h)

\displaystyle+\sum_{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}=1}(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}v)(\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{3}}h)+(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{1}}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{2}}v)(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\nabla_{\tilde{a}}\partial_{t}^{l_{3}}h)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}3e^{\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h\partial_{t}^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h+2\partial_{t}^{2}h(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h+\partial_{t}^{3}h(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h)

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+2e^{\prime\prime}(h)(3(\partial_{t}h)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h+3\partial_{t}^{2}h\partial_{t}h(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h)+e^{\prime\prime\prime}(h)(\partial_{t}h)^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h,

which gives

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\left(L_{41}+\partial_{t}^{3}(L_{1}+L_{2})\right)\partial_{t}^{4}h\,dy

(3.111)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}P\bigg(\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\partial_{t}^{4}h,(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}^{2}h\right)\right\|_{0},\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\left(\partial_{t}^{3}h,(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}\partial_{t}h\right)\right\|_{1},

\displaystyle\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}\left(\partial_{t}^{2}h,(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right)\right\|_{2},\left\|\partial_{t}h\right\|_{2},\left\|v_{t}\right\|_{3},\left\|v\right\|_{4},\left\|\eta\right\|_{4}\bigg).

Finally,

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\partial_{t}^{4}h\cdot\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}\partial_{t}^{3}L_{3}^{j}\,dt\right)\,dy

(3.112)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}\left\|\sqrt{e^{\prime}(h)}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}L_{3}^{j}\right\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}P\left(\|\tilde{a}\|_{2},\|\partial_{t}^{2}((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\eta,(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\tilde{\eta})\|_{1},\|v\|_{3},\|v_{t}\|_{2},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}\psi\|_{2},\|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\psi\|_{3}\right).

Summarizing (
3.106
)-(
3.112
), we get the energy estimates of
\partial_{t}^{3}
-differentiated wave equation

\frac{d}{dt}\left(\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{t}^{4}h\right\|_{0}^{2}\,dt+\left\|e^{\prime}(h)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{1}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left\|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{3}h\right\|_{0}^{2}\right)\lesssim P(\mathbb{E}_{\kappa}(t)).

(3.113)

3.4.2
Fully
(\mathbf{F}^{0}\cdot\partial)
-differentiated wave equation

We differentiate
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)
(no summation on
k,l
!) in the wave equation (
3.89
) to get

\displaystyle e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}^{2}h-\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\right)h

(3.114)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}e^{\prime}(h)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left[e^{\prime}(h),(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\right]\partial_{t}^{2}h+\left[(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial),\Delta_{\tilde{a}}\right]h+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\left[(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial),e^{\prime}(h)\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h}_{L_{42}}

\displaystyle+(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)(L_{1}+L_{2})-\sum_{j=1}^{3}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)L_{3}^{j}\,dt

Then we take the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
3.114
) and
(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h
to get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\right)\partial_{t}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\right)\,dy

(3.115)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\text{div}_{\tilde{a}}\left(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\partial_{t}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{3}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)^{2}h\right)\cdot\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\left(L_{42}+(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)(L_{1}+L_{2})\right)\cdot\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\cdot\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)\int_{0}^{T}L_{3}^{j}\right)\,dy

For LHS of (
3.115
), we integrate by part in the second term to get the energy term plus some commutators

\displaystyle\text{LHS of }\eqref{FFF0}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\left|(e^{\prime}(h))^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)\partial_{t}h\right|^{2}+\left|e^{\prime}(h)\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right|^{2}\,dy

(3.116)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{3}{2}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right|^{2}+e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)\partial_{t}h\left|\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right|^{2}\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}2e^{\prime}(h)e^{\prime\prime}(h)\left(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\partial_{t}\left((\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\,dy

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(e^{\prime}(h))^{2}\left(\nabla_{\tilde{a}}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\cdot\left(\left[\text{div}_{\tilde{a}},\partial_{t}\right](\mathbf{F}^{0}_{k}\cdot\partial)^{2}(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{l}\cdot\partial)h\right)\,dy.

The first term on the RHS of (
3.115
) gives the weight energy of
h
after we integrate
(\mathbf{F}^{0}_{j}\cdot\partial)
by parts for each
j
